con|agent_credentials|agent_credentials_check2|CHECK (((revoked_at IS NULL) = (revocation_reason IS NULL)))
con|agent_credentials|agent_credentials_check3|CHECK (((revoked_at IS NULL) OR (revoked_at >= issued_at)))
con|agent_credentials|agent_credentials_check|CHECK ((expires_at > issued_at))
con|agent_credentials|agent_credentials_credential_hash_check|CHECK ((credential_hash ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'::text))
con|agent_credentials|agent_credentials_organization_id_agent_id_fkey|FOREIGN KEY (organization_id, agent_id) REFERENCES agents(organization_id, agent_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
con|agent_credentials|agent_credentials_organization_id_issued_by_membership_id_fkey|FOREIGN KEY (organization_id, issued_by_membership_id) REFERENCES organization_memberships(organization_id, membership_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
con|agent_credentials|agent_credentials_organization_id_revoked_by_membership_id_fkey|FOREIGN KEY (organization_id, revoked_by_membership_id) REFERENCES organization_memberships(organization_id, membership_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
con|agent_credentials|agent_credentials_pkey|PRIMARY KEY (credential_id)
con|agent_credentials|agent_credentials_revocation_reason_check|CHECK ((revocation_reason = ANY (ARRAY[1, 2, 3, 4])))
con|agents|agents_agent_version_check|CHECK ((btrim(agent_version) <> ''::text))
con|agents|agents_installation_id_key|UNIQUE (installation_id)
con|agents|agents_name_check|CHECK ((btrim(name) <> ''::text))
con|agents|agents_organization_id_agent_id_key|UNIQUE (organization_id, agent_id)
con|agents|agents_organization_id_fkey|FOREIGN KEY (organization_id) REFERENCES organizations(organization_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
con|agents|agents_pkey|PRIMARY KEY (agent_id)
con|agents|agents_status_check|CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY[1, 2, 3, 4])))
con|auth_challenges|auth_challenges_attempts_check|CHECK (((attempts >= 0) AND (attempts <= 5)))
con|auth_challenges|auth_challenges_check1|CHECK (((consumed_at IS NULL) OR (invalidated_at IS NULL)))

    WHEN 1 THEN ((parameter_id IS NOT NULL) AND (parameter_value_id IS NULL) AND (secret_id IS NULL) AND (automation_version_id IS NULL) AND (binding_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 2 THEN ((parameter_id IS NOT NULL) AND (parameter_value_id IS NULL) AND (secret_id IS NULL) AND (automation_version_id IS NULL) AND (binding_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 3 THEN ((parameter_id IS NOT NULL) AND (parameter_value_id IS NOT NULL) AND (secret_id IS NULL) AND (automation_version_id IS NULL) AND (binding_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 4 THEN ((parameter_id IS NOT NULL) AND (parameter_value_id IS NULL) AND (secret_id IS NULL) AND (automation_version_id IS NULL) AND (binding_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 5 THEN ((secret_id IS NOT NULL) AND (parameter_id IS NULL) AND (parameter_value_id IS NULL) AND (automation_version_id IS NULL) AND (binding_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 6 THEN ((secret_id IS NOT NULL) AND (parameter_id IS NULL) AND (parameter_value_id IS NULL) AND (automation_version_id IS NULL) AND (binding_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 7 THEN ((secret_id IS NOT NULL) AND (parameter_id IS NULL) AND (parameter_value_id IS NULL) AND (automation_version_id IS NULL) AND (binding_id IS NULL))
    WHEN 8 THEN ((automation_version_id IS NOT NULL) AND (binding_id IS NOT NULL) AND (((parameter_id IS NOT NULL) AND (secret_id IS NULL)) OR ((parameter_id IS NULL) AND (secret_id IS NOT NULL))))
    WHEN 9 THEN ((automation_version_id IS NOT NULL) AND (binding_id IS NOT NULL) AND (((parameter_id IS NOT NULL) AND (secret_id IS NULL)) OR ((parameter_id IS NULL) AND (secret_id IS NOT NULL))))
    WHEN 10 THEN ((automation_version_id IS NOT NULL) AND (binding_id IS NOT NULL) AND (NOT ((parameter_id IS NOT NULL) AND (secret_id IS NOT NULL))))
    ELSE false
END IS TRUE))
